     1→"""Ground node: route entities via TerminologyRouter and select with MedGemma.
     2→
     3→This node is the fourth in the protocol processing pipeline:
     4→ingest→extract→parse→ground→persist
     5→It implements the delegation pattern: thin orchestration that calls tools.
     6→
     7→Per user decisions:
     8→- Error accumulation: entity failures are logged and accumulated, not fatal
     9→- Audit trail: all grounding decisions logged to AuditLog
    10→- TerminologyRouter handles entity-type-aware API routing
    11→- MedGemma selects the best match from candidates
    12→- Field mappings generated per entity for AutoCriteria decomposition
    13→- Consent entities skipped explicitly (not groundable to medical codes)
    14→- Demographics routed normally via umls/snomed (age/gender agentic reasoning)
    15→- Agentic retry loop: max 3 attempts, expert_review routing on exhaustion
    16→
    17→Architecture note: Graph nodes ARE allowed to import from api-service for DB access.
    18→"""
    19→
    20→from __future__ import annotations
    21→
    22→import asyncio
    23→import json
    24→import logging
    25→import os
    26→import re
    27→import time
    28→from typing import Any
    29→
    30→from api_service.storage import engine
    31→from rapidfuzz import fuzz
    32→from shared.models import AuditLog
    33→from sqlmodel import Session
    34→
    35→from protocol_processor.schemas.grounding import EntityGroundingResult
    36→from protocol_processor.state import PipelineState
    37→from protocol_processor.tools.field_mapper import generate_field_mappings
    38→from protocol_processor.tools.medgemma_decider import (
    39→    agentic_reasoning_loop,
    40→    medgemma_decide,
    41→)
    42→from protocol_processor.tools.omop_mapper import (
    43→    OmopLookupResult,
    44→    _get_omop_engine,
    45→    lookup_omop_concept,
    46→)
    47→from protocol_processor.tools.terminology_router import (
    48→    TerminologyRouter,
    49→    _is_likely_acronym,
    50→)
    51→from protocol_processor.tools.unit_normalizer import normalize_value
    52→
    53→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    54→
    55→# Singleton router instance (loads YAML config once)
    56→_router: TerminologyRouter | None = None
    57→
    58→
    59→def _get_router() -> TerminologyRouter:
    60→    """Get or create the singleton TerminologyRouter."""
    61→    global _router  # noqa: PLW0603
    62→    if _router is None:
    63→        _router = TerminologyRouter()
    64→    return _router
    65→
    66→
    67→def _reconcile_dual_grounding(
    68→    result: EntityGroundingResult,
    69→    omop_result: OmopLookupResult,
    70→) -> EntityGroundingResult:
    71→    """Reconcile TerminologyRouter + OMOP mapper results.
    72→
    73→    Sets omop_concept_id and reconciliation_status on the result:
    74→    - Both succeed → "agree" (high confidence) or "disagreement"
    75→    - Only TerminologyRouter → "omop_missing"
    76→    - Only OMOP → "tooluniverse_missing"
    77→    - Neither → no change (existing expert_review behavior)
    78→
    79→    Agreement is checked by comparing the OMOP concept_name against
    80→    the ToolUniverse preferred_term (fuzzy match).
    81→
    82→    Args:
    83→        result: EntityGroundingResult from TerminologyRouter path.
    84→        omop_result: OmopLookupResult from OMOP mapper path.
    85→
    86→    Returns:
    87→        The same EntityGroundingResult, mutated with OMOP fields.
    88→    """
    89→    tu_ok = result.selected_code is not None
    90→    omop_ok = omop_result.omop_concept_id is not None
    91→
    92→    if tu_ok and omop_ok:
    93→        result.omop_concept_id = omop_result.omop_concept_id
    94→        # Fuzzy agreement check using token_set_ratio (order-insensitive)
    95→        tu_term = (result.preferred_term or "").lower()
    96→        omop_term = (omop_result.omop_concept_name or "").lower()
    97→        if tu_term and omop_term and fuzz.token_set_ratio(tu_term, omop_term) >= 80:
    98→            result.reconciliation_status = "agree"
    99→        else:
   100→            result.reconciliation_status = "disagreement"
   101→        logger.info(
   102→            "Dual grounding for '%s': TU=%s, OMOP=%s, status=%s",
   103→            result.entity_text[:50],
   104→            result.selected_code,
   105→            omop_result.omop_concept_id,
   106→            result.reconciliation_status,
   107→        )
   108→    elif tu_ok and not omop_ok:
   109→        result.reconciliation_status = "omop_missing"
   110→    elif not tu_ok and omop_ok:
   111→        result.omop_concept_id = omop_result.omop_concept_id
   112→        result.reconciliation_status = "tooluniverse_missing"
   113→    # else: neither — leave as-is (expert_review path)
   114→
   115→    return result
   116→
   117→
   118→def _log_grounding_audit(
   119→    session: Session,
   120→    protocol_id: str,
   121→    criterion_id: str,
   122→    entity: dict[str, Any],
   123→    candidates: list[Any],
   124→    result: Any,
   125→) -> None:
   126→    """Create an AuditLog entry for a grounding decision.
   127→
   128→    Records full details: entity, all candidates considered, selected code,
   129→    reasoning, and confidence. Provides the audit trail per user decision:
   130→    "All agent exchanges (MedGemma decisions, API calls, routing) are logged."
   131→
   132→    Args:
   133→        session: Active SQLModel session.
   134→        protocol_id: Protocol ID for context.
   135→        criterion_id: Criterion ID this entity belongs to.
   136→        entity: Entity dict from entities_json.
   137→        candidates: GroundingCandidate objects from TerminologyRouter.
   138→        result: EntityGroundingResult from medgemma_decide.
   139→    """
   140→    audit = AuditLog(
   141→        event_type="entity_grounded",
   142→        target_type="criteria",
   143→        target_id=criterion_id,
   144→        details={
   145→            "protocol_id": protocol_id,
   146→            "entity_text": result.entity_text,
   147→            "entity_type": result.entity_type,
   148→            "candidate_count": len(candidates),
   149→            "candidates": [
   150→                {
   151→                    "source_api": c.source_api,
   152→                    "code": c.code,
   153→                    "preferred_term": c.preferred_term,
   154→                    "score": c.score,
   155→                }
   156→                for c in candidates
   157→            ],
   158→            "selected_code": result.selected_code,
   159→            "selected_system": result.selected_system,
   160→            "preferred_term": result.preferred_term,
   161→            "confidence": result.confidence,
   162→            "reasoning": result.reasoning,
   163→            "omop_concept_id": result.omop_concept_id,
   164→            "reconciliation_status": result.reconciliation_status,
   165→        },
   166→    )
   167→    session.add(audit)
   168→
   169→
   170→def _flush_audit_logs(
   171→    protocol_id: str,
   172→    pending_audits: list[tuple[str, dict[str, Any], list[Any], Any]],
   173→) -> None:
   174→    """Batch-write audit log entries in a single session.
   175→
   176→    Args:
   177→        protocol_id: Protocol ID for context.
   178→        pending_audits: List of (criterion_id, entity, candidates, result) tuples.
   179→    """
   180→    if not pending_audits:
   181→        return
   182→    try:
   183→        with Session(engine) as session:
   184→            for criterion_id, entity, candidates, result in pending_audits:
   185→                _log_grounding_audit(
   186→                    session,
   187→                    protocol_id,
   188→                    criterion_id,
   189→                    entity,
   190→                    candidates,
   191→                    result,
   192→                )
   193→            session.commit()
   194→    except Exception as audit_error:
   195→        logger.warning(
   196→            "Failed to batch-write %d AuditLog entries: %s",
   197→            len(pending_audits),
   198→            audit_error,
   199→        )
   200→
   201→
   202→async def _ground_entity_with_retry(
   203→    entity: dict[str, Any],
   204→    router: TerminologyRouter,
   205→    criterion_text: str,
   206→) -> tuple[EntityGroundingResult, int]:
   207→    """Ground a single entity with agentic retry loop (max 3 attempts).
   208→
   209→    Implements the MedGemma agentic reasoning loop:
   210→    1. Initial grounding via TerminologyRouter + MedGemma
   211→    2. If low confidence, ask MedGemma 3 questions (valid? derived? rephrase?)
   212→    3. Retry with reformulated query (up to 2 more attempts, 3 total)
   213→    4. After 3 failed attempts, mark for expert_review
   214→
   215→    Args:
   216→        entity: Entity dict with text, entity_type, criterion_id.
   217→        router: TerminologyRouter singleton for API dispatch.
   218→        criterion_text: Full criterion text for context.
   219→
   220→    Returns:
   221→        Tuple of (EntityGroundingResult, attempt_count). May have expert_review
   222→        marker in reasoning.
   223→    """
   224→    entity_text = entity.get("text", "")
   225→    entity_type = entity.get("entity_type", "")
   226→
   227→    # Step 1: Route entity through TerminologyRouter to get candidates
   228→    candidates = await router.route_entity(entity_text, entity_type)
   229→
   230→    # Log when TerminologyRouter returns empty
   231→    apis = router.get_apis_for_entity(entity_type)
   232→    if not candidates:
   233→        if not apis:
   234→            logger.info(
   235→                "Entity '%s' (type=%s) skipped by TerminologyRouter"
   236→                " — no APIs configured for this type",
   237→                entity_text[:50],
   238→                entity_type,
   239→            )
   240→            # No APIs configured → route directly to expert_review
   241→            return (
   242→                EntityGroundingResult(
   243→                    entity_text=entity_text,
   244→                    entity_type=entity_type,
   245→                    selected_code=None,
   246→                    selected_system=None,
   247→                    preferred_term=None,
   248→                    confidence=0.0,
   249→                    candidates=[],
   250→                    reasoning=(
   251→                        "[expert_review] No terminology APIs configured "
   252→                        f"for entity type '{entity_type}'. "
   253→                        "Routed to expert review."
   254→                    ),
   255→                ),
   256→                1,
   257→            )
   258→        else:
   259→            logger.info(
   260→                "Entity '%s' (type=%s) returned zero candidates from all APIs",
   261→                entity_text[:50],
   262→                entity_type,
   263→            )
   264→
   265→    # Step 2: MedGemma selects best match from candidates
   266→    result = await medgemma_decide(entity, candidates, criterion_text)
   267→
   268→    # Step 3: Agentic retry loop — max 3 attempts total
   269→    attempt = 1
   270→    current_query = entity_text
   271→    while (
   272→        result.selected_code is None
   273→        and result.confidence < 0.5
   274→        and attempt < 3
   275→        and router.get_apis_for_entity(entity_type)
   276→    ):
   277→        attempt += 1
   278→        reasoning_result = await agentic_reasoning_loop(
   279→            entity={**entity, "_previous_query": current_query},
   280→            criterion_context=criterion_text,
   281→            router=router,
   282→            attempt=attempt,
   283→        )
   284→
   285→        if reasoning_result.should_skip:
   286→            logger.info(
   287→                "Entity '%s' marked non-medical by agentic reasoning"
   288→                " (attempt %d) — skipping",
   289→                entity_text[:50],
   290→                attempt,
   291→            )
   292→            return (
   293→                EntityGroundingResult(
   294→                    entity_text=entity_text,
   295→                    entity_type=entity_type,
   296→                    selected_code=None,
   297→                    selected_system=None,
   298→                    preferred_term=None,
   299→                    confidence=0.0,
   300→                    candidates=[],
   301→                    reasoning=(
   302→                        f"Skipped by agentic reasoning (attempt {attempt}): "
   303→                        f"{reasoning_result.reasoning}"
   304→                    ),
   305→                ),
   306→                attempt,
   307→            )
   308→
   309→        new_query = (
   310→            reasoning_result.rephrased_query
   311→            or reasoning_result.derived_term
   312→            or reasoning_result.gemini_suggestion
   313→            or current_query
   314→        )
   315→        current_query = new_query
   316→
   317→        logger.info(
   318→            "Agentic retry (attempt %d) for '%s' using query '%s'",
   319→            attempt,
   320→            entity_text[:50],
   321→            new_query[:50],
   322→        )
   323→
   324→        new_candidates = await router.route_entity(new_query, entity_type)
   325→        if new_candidates:
   326→            retry_entity = {**entity, "text": new_query}
   327→            result = await medgemma_decide(retry_entity, new_candidates, criterion_text)
   328→            if result.selected_code and result.confidence >= 0.5:
   329→                # Success — preserve original entity_text
   330→                return (
   331→                    EntityGroundingResult(
   332→                        entity_text=entity_text,
   333→                        entity_type=result.entity_type,
   334→                        selected_code=result.selected_code,
   335→                        selected_system=result.selected_system,
   336→                        preferred_term=result.preferred_term,
   337→                        confidence=result.confidence,
   338→                        candidates=result.candidates,
   339→                        reasoning=(
   340→                            f"[Attempt {attempt}, query='{new_query}'] "
   341→                            f"{result.reasoning}"
   342→                        ),
   343→                        field_mappings=result.field_mappings,
   344→                    ),
   345→                    attempt,
   346→                )
   347→
   348→    # Route to expert_review if all attempts exhausted without success
   349→    if result.selected_code is None and attempt >= 2:
   350→        logger.warning(
   351→            "Entity '%s' (type=%s) routed to expert_review after"
   352→            " %d failed grounding attempts",
   353→            entity_text[:50],
   354→            entity_type,
   355→            attempt,
   356→        )
   357→        return (
   358→            EntityGroundingResult(
   359→                entity_text=result.entity_text,
   360→                entity_type=result.entity_type,
   361→                selected_code=result.selected_code,
   362→                selected_system=result.selected_system,
   363→                preferred_term=result.preferred_term,
   364→                confidence=result.confidence,
   365→                candidates=result.candidates,
   366→                reasoning=(
   367→                    f"[expert_review] Routed to expert review after "
   368→                    f"{attempt} failed grounding attempts. "
   369→                    f"Last reasoning: {result.reasoning}"
   370→                ),
   371→            ),
   372→            attempt,
   373→        )
   374→
   375→    return (result, attempt)
   376→
   377→
   378→def _ground_categorical_values(
   379→    mappings: list[dict[str, Any]],
   380→) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
   381→    """Ground categorical values in field mappings using unit_normalizer.
   382→
   383→    For standard-type mappings with non-numeric values (e.g. "Severe",
   384→    "positive", "NYHA Class III"), attempt to resolve a value_concept_id
   385→    using the unit_normalizer's value lookup.
   386→
   387→    This is a best-effort post-processing step — failures don't block
   388→    the grounding result.
   389→
   390→    Args:
   391→        mappings: List of field mapping dicts from generate_field_mappings.
   392→
   393→    Returns:
   394→        The same list with value_concept_id populated where possible.
   395→    """
   396→    numeric_re = re.compile(r"^[\d.,\-]+$")
   397→
   398→    for mapping in mappings:
   399→        try:
   400→            value_obj = mapping.get("value", {})
   401→            if not isinstance(value_obj, dict):
   402→                continue
   403→
   404→            # Only process standard-type values
   405→            if value_obj.get("type") != "standard":
   406→                continue
   407→
   408→            val_text = value_obj.get("value")
   409→            if not val_text or not isinstance(val_text, str):
   410→                continue
   411→
   412→            # Skip numeric values — they don't need concept grounding
   413→            if numeric_re.match(val_text.strip()):
   414→                continue
   415→
   416→            # Already grounded
   417→            if mapping.get("value_concept_id"):
   418→                continue
   419→
   420→            # Try unit_normalizer's categorical value lookup (fast path)
   421→            normalized_text, concept_id = normalize_value(val_text)
   422→            if concept_id is not None:
   423→                mapping["value_concept_id"] = str(concept_id)
   424→                mapping["value_concept_system"] = "OMOP"
   425→                logger.debug(
   426→                    "Grounded categorical value '%s' → concept_id=%s",
   427→                    val_text,
   428→                    concept_id,
   429→                )
   430→        except Exception as e:
   431→            logger.debug("Categorical value grounding failed for mapping: %s", e)
   432→
   433→    return mappings
   434→
   435→
   436→async def _ground_entity_parallel(
   437→    entity: dict[str, Any],
   438→    router: TerminologyRouter,
   439→    criterion_text: str,
   440→    entity_num: int,
   441→    total: int,
   442→    semaphore: asyncio.Semaphore,
   443→) -> tuple[EntityGroundingResult | None, str | None, float, int]:
   444→    """Ground a single entity with concurrency control and timing.
   445→
   446→    Acquires the semaphore before grounding to cap concurrent API calls.
   447→    Returns a (result, error) tuple for error isolation -- one entity failure
   448→    does not crash others in the asyncio.gather batch.
   449→
   450→    Args:
   451→        entity: Entity dict with text, entity_type, criterion_id.
   452→        router: TerminologyRouter singleton for API dispatch.
   453→        criterion_text: Full criterion text for context.
   454→        entity_num: 1-based index for logging.
   455→        total: Total entity count for logging.
   456→        semaphore: Concurrency limiter (Semaphore(4)).
   457→
   458→    Returns:
   459→        (EntityGroundingResult, None, elapsed_ms, retry_attempts) on success,
   460→        or (None, error_message, elapsed_ms, 0) on failure.
   461→    """
   462→    async with semaphore:
   463→        entity_text = entity.get("text", "")[:50]
   464→        entity_type = entity.get("entity_type", "")
   465→        start = time.monotonic()
   466→        logger.info(
   467→            "Grounding entity %d/%d: '%s' (type=%s) — start",
   468→            entity_num,
   469→            total,
   470→            entity_text,
   471→            entity_type,
   472→        )
   473→        try:
   474→            # Dual grounding: run TerminologyRouter + OMOP mapper
   475→            # in parallel per entity (Phase 1a)
   476→            full_entity_text = entity.get("text", "")
   477→            acronym = _is_likely_acronym(full_entity_text)
   478→            tu_task = _ground_entity_with_retry(entity, router, criterion_text)
   479→            omop_task = lookup_omop_concept(
   480→                full_entity_text, entity_type, is_acronym=acronym
   481→            )
   482→            (result, retry_attempts), omop_result = await asyncio.gather(
   483→                tu_task, omop_task
   484→            )
   485→
   486→            # Reconcile dual grounding results
   487→            result = _reconcile_dual_grounding(result, omop_result)
   488→
   489→            # Thread criterion_id from parse into result
   490→            criterion_id = entity.get("criterion_id")
   491→            if criterion_id:
   492→                result.criterion_id = criterion_id
   493→
   494→            # Generate field mappings (same semaphore slot)
   495→            field_mappings = await generate_field_mappings(result, criterion_text)
   496→
   497→            # Post-process: ground categorical values in field mappings
   498→            if field_mappings:
   499→                field_mappings = _ground_categorical_values(field_mappings)
   500→
   501→            result.field_mappings = field_mappings if field_mappings else None
   502→
   503→            elapsed = time.monotonic() - start
   504→            elapsed_ms = elapsed * 1000
   505→            logger.info(
   506→                "Entity %d/%d '%s' grounded in %.1fs: code=%s, omop=%s, conf=%.2f",
   507→                entity_num,
   508→                total,
   509→                entity_text,
   510→                elapsed,
   511→                result.selected_code or "N/A",
   512→                result.omop_concept_id or "N/A",
   513→                result.confidence,
   514→            )
   515→            return (result, None, elapsed_ms, retry_attempts)
   516→        except Exception as e:
   517→            elapsed = time.monotonic() - start
   518→            elapsed_ms = elapsed * 1000
   519→            logger.error(
   520→                "Entity %d/%d '%s' failed in %.1fs: %s",
   521→                entity_num,
   522→                total,
   523→                entity_text,
   524→                elapsed,
   525→                e,
   526→            )
   527→            return (None, str(e), elapsed_ms, 0)
   528→
   529→
   530→async def ground_node(state: PipelineState) -> dict[str, Any]:  # noqa: C901
   531→    """Ground entities via TerminologyRouter + MedGemma.
   532→
   533→    Parses entities_json from state, routes each entity through TerminologyRouter
   534→    to get terminology candidates, then passes candidates to medgemma_decide for
   535→    best-match selection. Generates field mappings for each successfully grounded
   536→    entity.
   537→
   538→    Error accumulation: individual entity failures are logged and collected in
   539→    errors list. Processing continues with remaining entities. Only a total
   540→    failure to parse entities_json triggers a fatal error.
   541→
   542→    Args:
   543→        state: Current pipeline state with entities_json and protocol_id.
   544→
   545→    Returns:
   546→        Dict with grounded_entities_json and accumulated errors list,
   547→        or fatal error dict if entities_json cannot be parsed.
   548→    """
   549→    from protocol_processor.tracing import pipeline_span
   550→
   551→    if state.get("error"):
   552→        return {}
   553→
   554→    protocol_id = state.get("protocol_id", "")
   555→    with pipeline_span(
   556→        "ground_node", span_type="TOOL", protocol_id=protocol_id
   557→    ) as span:
   558→        span.set_inputs({"protocol_id": state.get("protocol_id", "")})
   559→
   560→        try:
   561→            entities_json = state.get("entities_json")
   562→            if not entities_json:
   563→                span.set_outputs({"error": "No entities_json in state"})
   564→                return {
   565→                    "error": "No entities_json in state — parse node may have failed",
   566→                }
   567→
   568→            entity_items: list[dict[str, Any]] = json.loads(entities_json)
   569→            protocol_id = state["protocol_id"]
   570→
   571→            span.set_inputs(
   572→                {
   573→                    "protocol_id": protocol_id,
   574→                    "entity_count": len(entity_items),
   575→                }
   576→            )
   577→
   578→            logger.info(
   579→                "Starting grounding for protocol %s: %d entities",
   580→                protocol_id,
   581→                len(entity_items),
   582→            )
   583→
   584→            # Fail fast: verify OMOP vocabulary is reachable before
   585→            # starting entity grounding. No silent fallback.
   586→            _get_omop_engine()
   587→
   588→            router = _get_router()
   589→            accumulated_errors: list[str] = list(state.get("errors") or [])
   590→            grounding_results: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
   591→
   592→            # Pre-process: handle consent entities synchronously (skip grounding)
   593→            entities_to_ground: list[tuple[int, dict[str, Any]]] = []
   594→            for idx, entity in enumerate(entity_items, 1):
   595→                entity_text = entity.get("text", "")
   596→                entity_type = entity.get("entity_type", "")
   597→
   598→                if entity_type == "Consent":
   599→                    logger.info(
   600→                        "Entity '%s' (type=Consent) skipped — consent/participation"
   601→                        " criteria are not groundable to terminology codes",
   602→                        entity_text[:50],
   603→                    )
   604→                    consent_result = EntityGroundingResult(
   605→                        entity_text=entity_text,
   606→                        entity_type=entity_type,
   607→                        selected_code=None,
   608→                        selected_system=None,
   609→                        preferred_term=None,
   610→                        confidence=0.0,
   611→                        candidates=[],
   612→                        reasoning=(
   613→                            "Consent/participation criterion — not groundable"
   614→                            " to medical terminology codes"
   615→                        ),
   616→                        criterion_id=entity.get("criterion_id"),
   617→                    )
   618→                    grounding_results.append(consent_result.model_dump())
   619→                else:
   620→                    entities_to_ground.append((idx, entity))
   621→
   622→            # Dev/debug: truncate entity list for faster runs and fewer API calls.
   623→            # DEV_MAX_ENTITIES takes precedence over PIPELINE_MAX_ENTITIES when set.
   624→            _max_entities = int(
   625→                os.getenv("DEV_MAX_ENTITIES")
   626→                or os.getenv("PIPELINE_MAX_ENTITIES")
   627→                or "0"
   628→            )
   629→            if _max_entities > 0:
   630→                entities_to_ground = entities_to_ground[:_max_entities]
   631→                logger.info(
   632→                    "Max entities limit=%d: truncated to %d entities",
   633→                    _max_entities,
   634→                    len(entities_to_ground),
   635→                )
   636→
   637→            # Pre-flight warmup: absorb MedGemma cold-start latency before
   638→            # the parallel batch. Non-fatal — if warmup fails, entities proceed.
   639→            try:
   640→                from langchain_core.messages import HumanMessage as HumanMessageType
   641→
   642→                from protocol_processor.tools.medgemma_decider import (
   643→                    _get_medgemma_model,
   644→                )
   645→
   646→                warmup_model = _get_medgemma_model()
   647→                await warmup_model.ainvoke([HumanMessageType(content="ready")])
   648→                logger.info("MedGemma warmup succeeded")
   649→            except Exception as warmup_err:
   650→                logger.info("MedGemma warmup skipped: %s", warmup_err)
   651→
   652→            # Parallel grounding with asyncio.gather + semaphore
   653→            # Semaphore created here (not module-level) to bind to current event loop
   654→            semaphore = asyncio.Semaphore(4)
   655→            num_to_ground = len(entities_to_ground)
   656→
   657→            tasks = [
   658→                _ground_entity_parallel(
   659→                    entity,
   660→                    router,
   661→                    entity.get("criterion_text") or entity.get("text", ""),
   662→                    pos,
   663→                    num_to_ground,
   664→                    semaphore,
   665→                )
   666→                for pos, (idx, entity) in enumerate(entities_to_ground, 1)
   667→            ]
   668→
   669→            outcomes = await asyncio.gather(*tasks)
   670→
   671→            # Process outcomes and collect audit entries for batch write
   672→            pending_audits: list[tuple[str, dict[str, Any], list[Any], Any]] = []
   673→            entity_times: list[float] = []
   674→            total_retry_count = 0
   675→            for (idx, entity), (result, error, elapsed_ms, retries) in zip(
   676→                entities_to_ground, outcomes
   677→            ):
   678→                entity_times.append(elapsed_ms)
   679→                total_retry_count += max(0, retries - 1)  # retries beyond first attempt
   680→                entity_text = entity.get("text", "")
   681→                criterion_id = entity.get("criterion_id", "")
   682→
   683→                if result is not None:
   684→                    pending_audits.append(
   685→                        (criterion_id, entity, result.candidates, result)
   686→                    )
   687→                    grounding_results.append(result.model_dump())
   688→                else:
   689→                    # Entity failed — create expert_review fallback
   690→                    cid_short = criterion_id[:12] if criterion_id else "unknown"
   691→                    error_msg = (
   692→                        f"Entity grounding failed for '{entity_text[:50]}'"
   693→                        f" (criterion={cid_short}): {error}"
   694→                    )
   695→                    logger.error(error_msg)
   696→                    accumulated_errors.append(error_msg)
   697→
   698→            # Batch-write all audit log entries in a single session
   699→            _flush_audit_logs(protocol_id, pending_audits)
   700→
   701→            logger.info(
   702→                "Ground node complete for protocol %s: %d grounded, %d errors",
   703→                protocol_id,
   704→                len(grounding_results),
   705→                len(accumulated_errors),
   706→            )
   707→
   708→            span.set_outputs(
   709→                {
   710→                    "grounded_count": len(grounding_results),
   711→                    "error_count": len(accumulated_errors),
   712→                    "avg_entity_ms": (
   713→                        round(sum(entity_times) / len(entity_times))
   714→                        if entity_times
   715→                        else 0
   716→                    ),
   717→                    "max_entity_ms": round(max(entity_times)) if entity_times else 0,
   718→                    "retry_count": total_retry_count,
   719→                }
   720→            )
   721→
   722→            return {
   723→                "grounded_entities_json": json.dumps(grounding_results),
   724→                "errors": accumulated_errors,
   725→            }
   726→
   727→        except Exception as e:
   728→            logger.exception(
   729→                "Ground node fatal error for protocol %s: %s",
   730→                state.get("protocol_id", "unknown"),
   731→                e,
   732→            )
   733→            span.set_outputs({"error": str(e)})
   734→            return {"error": f"Ground node failed: {e}"}
   735→
```

All checks pass. Here is a summary of every change made to `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/nodes/ground.py`.

---

### Changes made to `_ground_categorical_values`

**1. Function signature — added `# noqa: C901`** (line 378)

The two new `if` branches pushed the cyclomatic complexity from 11 to 13, triggering ruff's C901 rule. The suppression matches the identical pattern already used on `ground_node` elsewhere in the file.

```python
def _ground_categorical_values(  # noqa: C901
```

**2. Added `_value_norm` map after `numeric_re`** (lines 398-409)

```python
# Safety-net: normalize common LLM-generated categorical values
# to canonical boolean form for expression-tree consistency.
_value_norm = {
    "present": "True",
    "confirmed": "True",
    "positive": "True",
    "yes": "True",
    "absent": "False",
    "negative": "False",
    "not present": "False",
    "no": "False",
}
```

**3. Added value normalization logic inside the for-loop** (lines 429-446), inserted after the numeric skip (`continue`) and before the "already grounded" check:

```python
# Normalize "present"/"confirmed"/"absent" → "True"/"False"
canonical = _value_norm.get(val_text.strip().lower())
if canonical is not None:
    value_obj["value"] = canonical
    val_text = canonical
    logger.debug(
        "Normalized categorical value '%s' → '%s'",
        val_text,
        canonical,
    )

# If value was normalized to boolean, also normalize relation
if canonical is not None:
    rel = mapping.get("relation", "")
    if rel in ("contains", "has", "is"):
        mapping["relation"] = "="
    elif rel in ("not_contains", "not"):
        mapping["relation"] = "!="
```

The normalization mutates `value_obj["value"]` in-place (since `value_obj` is a reference into the mapping dict) and updates the local `val_text` variable so the subsequent `normalize_value()` OMOP lookup operates on the already-canonicalized string. The relation normalization then coerces vague relations like `"is"` / `"has"` to `"="` and `"not"` to `"!="` whenever the value was boolean-normalized.

> AGENT

## Summary of Changes

**68/68 tests pass.** Here's what was changed across 4 files:

### 1. `field_mapper.py` — Prompt fix (root cause)
- Added **CRITICAL boolean normalization rules**: conditions that are present/confirmed → `relation="=", value="True"`; conditions that are absent/excluded → `relation="!=", value="True"`
- Added explicit **"NEVER use" directive** for `"present"`, `"absent"`, `"confirmed"`, `"positive"`, `"negative"` as value strings
- Changed **fallback from `"confirmed"` to `"True"`**
- Added **anti-pattern examples** showing exactly what NOT to produce

### 2. `ground.py` — Safety-net normalization (defense in depth)
- Added `_value_norm` map in `_ground_categorical_values` that normalizes `"present"/"confirmed"/"positive"/"yes"` → `"True"` and `"absent"/"negative"/"not present"/"no"` → `"False"`
- Also normalizes **relations**: `"contains"/"has"/"is"` → `"="` and `"not_contains"/"not"` → `"!="` when the value was boolean-normalized
- Runs post-processing on every field mapping, catching any LLM output that slips through the prompt

### 3. `test_snippets.json` — Golden fixture normalization
- Snippets 3, 4, 5, 6: `"=="` → `"="`, `"present"`/`"confirmed"` → `"True"`
- Snippets 0-2, 7-8: unchanged (already correct)

### 4. `test_grounding_snippets.py` — New golden tests
- Added `test_venous_blood_snippet` (snippet 7) and `test_non_pregnant_snippet` (snippet 8)
- Added boolean normalization assertions to `test_ankylosing_spondylitis_snippet` and `test_female_surgically_sterile_snippet`

Now I have a complete picture. Here is a thorough breakdown of everything found:

---

## Frontend Criteria Page - Complete Analysis

The frontend lives entirely in `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/apps/hitl-ui/src/`.

---

### 1. All Frontend Files Related to the Criteria Page

**Primary screen:**
- `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/apps/hitl-ui/src/screens/CriteriaSpreadsheet.tsx`

**Criteria table sub-components:**
- `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/apps/hitl-ui/src/components/criteria-table/CriteriaTable.tsx`
- `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/apps/hitl-ui/src/components/criteria-table/columns.tsx`
- `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/apps/hitl-ui/src/components/criteria-table/filters.tsx`
- `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/apps/hitl-ui/src/components/criteria-table/ConfidenceBadge.tsx`
- `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/apps/hitl-ui/src/components/criteria-table/ExpressionTreeCell.tsx`
- `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/apps/hitl-ui/src/components/criteria-table/FieldMappingCell.tsx`

**Data fetching hook:**
- `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/apps/hitl-ui/src/hooks/useCriteriaSpreadsheet.ts`

**Route definition (in App.tsx):**
- `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/apps/hitl-ui/src/App.tsx` — route `/criteria` maps to `<CriteriaSpreadsheet />`

---

### 2. Routing

In `App.tsx`:
```tsx
<Route
    path="/criteria"
    element={
        <RequireAuth>
            <CriteriaSpreadsheet />
        </RequireAuth>
    }
/>
```
The nav link is:
```tsx
<Link to="/criteria" className="...">Criteria</Link>
```

---

### 3. The Criteria Page Component (`CriteriaSpreadsheet.tsx`)

The page manages three filter states locally — `criteriaType`, `category`, and `minConfidence` — plus a `page` state. It calls `useCriteriaSpreadsheet(filters)` to load data from the backend:

```tsx
const { data, isLoading, error } = useCriteriaSpreadsheet({
    page,
    pageSize: 50,
    criteriaType: criteriaType || undefined,
    category: category || undefined,
    minConfidence: minConfidence ? Number.parseFloat(minConfidence) : undefined,
    sortBy: 'confidence',
    sortOrder: 'desc',
});
```

It renders a stat summary (inclusion count, exclusion count, avg confidence, grounded count), then a `<FilterBar>`, then a `<CriteriaTable>`, then pagination buttons.

**Key observation: there is NO protocol selector/dropdown on the criteria page.** The `useCriteriaSpreadsheet` hook accepts a `protocolId` filter, but `CriteriaSpreadsheet.tsx` never initializes or passes that state. The data is fetched across all protocols.

---

### 4. The `FilterBar` Component (`filters.tsx`)

The `FilterBar` contains three controls:
- A native `<select>` for **criteria type** (inclusion / exclusion) — hardcoded list `CRITERIA_TYPES`
- A native `<select>` for **category** — hardcoded list `CATEGORIES` (medical_history, lab_values, medications, procedures, demographics, other)
- A number `<input>` for **minimum confidence** (0.0–1.0)

There is no protocol dropdown here. Its props interface is:
```tsx
interface FilterBarProps {
    criteriaType: string;
    setCriteriaType: (v: string) => void;
    category: string;
    setCategory: (v: string) => void;
    minConfidence: string;
    setMinConfidence: (v: string) => void;
}
```

---

### 5. The Data Hook (`useCriteriaSpreadsheet.ts`)

The `CriteriaFilters` interface **does** include `protocolId` as an optional field:
```ts
export interface CriteriaFilters {
    page: number;
    pageSize: number;
    criteriaType?: string;
    category?: string;
    protocolId?: string;       // <-- supported but never used by the screen
    minConfidence?: number;
    sortBy?: string;
    sortOrder?: 'asc' | 'desc';
    hasGrounding?: boolean;    // <-- also supported but never used by the screen
}
```

When `protocolId` is set, it adds `protocol_id` to the query string and calls:
```
GET /criteria/structured?page=...&protocol_id=<id>&...
```

---

### 6. Protocol Loading — How It Works Elsewhere

The protocol list/selection is handled by `useProtocols.ts` and used in other screens. The relevant hooks are:

- `useProtocolList(page, pageSize, status?, deduplicate?)` — fetches `GET /protocols?page=...&page_size=...` and returns `{ items: Protocol[], total, page, page_size, pages }`
- `useProtocol(id)` — fetches a single protocol by ID from `GET /protocols/:id`

The `Protocol` interface:
```ts
export interface Protocol {
    id: string;
    title: string;
    file_uri: string;
    status: 'uploaded' | 'extracting' | ... | 'complete' | 'archived';
    error_reason: string | null;
    page_count: number | null;
    quality_score: number | null;
    metadata_: Record<string, unknown>;
    created_at: string;
    updated_at: string;
    version_count?: number;
}
```

The `ProtocolList` screen (`/screens/ProtocolList.tsx`) uses `useProtocolList` and renders a table with status-filter chip buttons. There is no reusable protocol selector/dropdown component — it is all inline in that screen.

---

### Summary

**The criteria page currently has no protocol selector.** The infrastructure to filter by protocol exists in the hook (`protocolId` is a supported filter param, sent as `?protocol_id=<id>` to `GET /criteria/structured`), but:
1. `CriteriaSpreadsheet.tsx` never creates a `protocolId` state variable
2. `FilterBar` does not have a protocol prop or dropdown
3. No protocol selector component was built and wired in

To add a protocol selector, you would need to:
1. Add `protocolId` state to `CriteriaSpreadsheet.tsx` and pass it into `useCriteriaSpreadsheet`
2. Add a protocol dropdown to `FilterBar` (or a new component) that calls `useProtocolList` to populate options
3. Extend `FilterBarProps` to include `protocolId` / `setProtocolId`

    10→export default function CriteriaSpreadsheet() {
    11→    const [page, setPage] = useState(1);
    12→    const [criteriaType, setCriteriaType] = useState('');

    17→        page,

    25→

    32→    const exclusionCount = items.filter((i) => i.criteria_type === 'exclusion').length;
    33→    const avgConfidence =
    34→        items.length > 0 ? items.reduce((s, i) => s + i.confidence, 0) / items.length : 0;

    36→
    37→    return (
    38→        <div className="container mx-auto p-6 space-y-6">

    62→                </div>
    63→                <div className="rounded-lg border bg-card p-4">
    64→                    <div className="text-2xl font-bold text-red-600 tabular-nums">
    65→                        {exclusionCount}
    66→                    </div>
    67→                    <div className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Exclusion (this page)</div>
    68→                </div>
    69→                <div className="rounded-lg border bg-card p-4">
    70→                    <div className="text-2xl font-bold text-blue-600 tabular-nums">
    71→                        {Math.round(avgConfidence * 100)}%
    72→                    </div>

    74→                </div>

   127→                            variant="outline"

   143→                        </Button>
   144→                    </div>
   145→                </div>

    60→                {CRITERIA_TYPES.map((t) => (
    61→                    <option key={t} value={t}>
    62→                        {t.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + t.slice(1)}
    63→                    </option>
    64→                ))}
    65→            </select>
    66→
    67→            <select
    68→                value={category}
    69→                onChange={(e) => setCategory(e.target.value)}
    70→                className="rounded-md border border-input bg-background px-3 py-1.5 text-sm ring-offset-background focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-ring"
    71→            >
    72→                <option value="">All categories</option>
    73→                {CATEGORIES.map((c) => (
    74→                    <option key={c} value={c}>
    75→                        {c.replace(/_/g, ' ')}
    76→                    </option>
    77→                ))}
    78→            </select>
    79→
    80→            <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
    81→                <label htmlFor="min-confidence" className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
    82→                    Min confidence:
    83→                </label>
    84→                <input
    85→                    id="min-confidence"
    86→                    type="number"
    87→                    min="0"
    88→                    max="1"
    89→                    step="0.1"
    90→                    value={minConfidence}
    91→                    onChange={(e) => setMinConfidence(e.target.value)}
    92→                    className="w-16 rounded-md border border-input bg-background px-2 py-1.5 text-sm ring-offset-background focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-ring"
    93→                    placeholder="0.0"
    94→                />
    95→            </div>
    96→
    97→            {/* Active filter chips */}
    98→            <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
    99→                {criteriaType && (
   100→                    <FilterChip
   101→                        label="Type"
   102→                        value={criteriaType}
   103→                        onRemove={() => setCriteriaType('')}
   104→                    />
   105→                )}
   106→                {category && (
   107→                    <FilterChip
   108→                        label="Category"
   109→                        value={category.replace(/_/g, ' ')}
   110→                        onRemove={() => setCategory('')}
   111→                    />
   112→                )}
   113→                {minConfidence && (

   116→                        value={minConfidence}
   117→                        onRemove={() => setMinConfidence('')}
   118→                    />
   119→                )}
   120→            </div>
   121→        </div>
   122→    );
   123→}

     3→import { fetchApi } from '../lib/fetchApi';
     4→
     5→export interface EntitySummary {
     6→    id: string;
     7→    entity_type: string;
     8→    text: string;
     9→    grounding_code: string | null;
    10→    grounding_system: string | null;
    11→    preferred_term: string | null;
    12→    grounding_confidence: number | null;
    13→}
    14→
    15→export interface StructuredCriterionRow {
    16→    id: string;
    17→    protocol_id: string | null;
    18→    protocol_title: string | null;
    19→    text: string;
    20→    criteria_type: string;
    21→    category: string | null;
    22→    confidence: number;
    23→    assertion_status: string | null;
    24→    entities: EntitySummary[];
    25→    field_mappings: Array<Record<string, unknown>>;
    26→    structured_criterion: Record<string, unknown> | null;
    27→    review_status: string | null;
    28→    page_number: number | null;
    29→    source_section: string | null;
    30→}
    31→
    32→export interface StructuredCriteriaResponse {
    33→    items: StructuredCriterionRow[];
    34→    total: number;
    35→    page: number;
    36→    page_size: number;
    37→    pages: number;
    38→}
    39→
    40→export interface CriteriaFilters {
    41→    page: number;
    42→    pageSize: number;
    43→    criteriaType?: string;
    44→    category?: string;
    45→    protocolId?: string;
    46→    minConfidence?: number;
    47→    sortBy?: string;
    48→    sortOrder?: 'asc' | 'desc';
    49→    hasGrounding?: boolean;
    50→}
    51→
    52→export function useCriteriaSpreadsheet(filters: CriteriaFilters) {
    53→    const params = new URLSearchParams({
    54→        page: String(filters.page),
    55→        page_size: String(filters.pageSize),
    56→    });
    57→
    58→    if (filters.criteriaType) params.set('criteria_type', filters.criteriaType);
    59→    if (filters.category) params.set('category', filters.category);
    60→    if (filters.protocolId) params.set('protocol_id', filters.protocolId);
    61→    if (filters.minConfidence !== undefined)
    62→        params.set('min_confidence', String(filters.minConfidence));
    63→    if (filters.sortBy) params.set('sort_by', filters.sortBy);
    64→    if (filters.sortOrder) params.set('sort_order', filters.sortOrder);
    65→    if (filters.hasGrounding !== undefined)
    66→        params.set('has_grounding', String(filters.hasGrounding));
    67→
    68→    return useQuery({
    69→        queryKey: ['criteria-structured', filters],
    70→        queryFn: () =>
    71→            fetchApi<StructuredCriteriaResponse>(`/criteria/structured?${params.toString()}`),
    72→        staleTime: 30_000,
    73→    });
    74→}
    75→

     1→import { type UseQueryOptions, useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
     2→
     3→import { fetchApi } from '../lib/fetchApi';
     4→
     5→// --- TypeScript interfaces ---
     6→
     7→export interface Protocol {
     8→    id: string;
     9→    title: string;
    10→    file_uri: string;
    11→    status:
    12→        | 'uploaded'
    13→        | 'extracting'
    14→        | 'extraction_failed'
    15→        | 'grounding'
    16→        | 'grounding_failed'
    17→        | 'pending_review'
    18→        | 'processing'
    19→        | 'complete'
    20→        | 'dead_letter'
    21→        | 'archived';
    22→    error_reason: string | null;
    23→    page_count: number | null;
    24→    quality_score: number | null;
    25→    metadata_: Record<string, unknown>;
    26→    created_at: string;
    27→    updated_at: string;
    28→    version_count?: number; // Present when deduplicate=true
    29→}
    30→
    31→export interface ProtocolListResponse {
    32→    items: Protocol[];
    33→    total: number;
    34→    page: number;
    35→    page_size: number;
    36→    pages: number;
    37→}
    38→
    39→export interface UploadResponse {
    40→    protocol_id: string;
    41→    upload_url: string;
    42→    gcs_path: string;
    43→}
    44→
    45→// --- Hooks ---
    46→
    47→export function useProtocolList(
    48→    page: number,
    49→    pageSize: number,
    50→    status?: string,
    51→    deduplicate?: boolean
    52→) {
    53→    const params = new URLSearchParams({
    54→        page: String(page),
    55→        page_size: String(pageSize),
    56→    });
    57→    if (status) {
    58→        params.set('status', status);
    59→    }
    60→    if (deduplicate) {
    61→        params.set('deduplicate', 'true');
    62→    }
    63→
    64→    return useQuery({
    65→        queryKey: ['protocols', page, pageSize, status, deduplicate],
    66→        queryFn: () => fetchApi<ProtocolListResponse>(`/protocols?${params.toString()}`),
    67→    });
    68→}
    69→
    70→export function useProtocol(id: string, options?: Partial<UseQueryOptions<Protocol, Error>>) {
    71→    return useQuery({
    72→        queryKey: ['protocols', id],
    73→        queryFn: () => fetchApi<Protocol>(`/protocols/${id}`),
    74→        enabled: Boolean(id),
    75→        ...options,
    76→    });
    77→}
    78→
    79→export function useUploadProtocol() {
    80→    const queryClient = useQueryClient();
    81→
    82→    return useMutation({
    83→        mutationFn: async ({ file }: { file: File }) => {
    84→            // Step 1: Get signed upload URL from backend
    85→            const uploadResp = await fetchApi<UploadResponse>('/protocols/upload', {
    86→                method: 'POST',
    87→                body: JSON.stringify({
    88→                    filename: file.name,
    89→                    content_type: file.type,
    90→                    file_size_bytes: file.size,
    91→                }),
    92→            });
    93→
    94→            // Step 2: Upload file directly to GCS via signed URL
    95→            const putResp = await fetch(uploadResp.upload_url, {
    96→                method: 'PUT',
    97→                headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/pdf' },
    98→                body: file,
    99→            });
   100→
   101→            if (!putResp.ok) {
   102→                throw new Error(`Upload failed: ${putResp.status} ${putResp.statusText}`);
   103→            }
   104→
   105→            // Step 3: Read file as base64 for quality scoring, then confirm
   106→            const arrayBuffer = await file.arrayBuffer();
   107→            const bytes = new Uint8Array(arrayBuffer);
   108→            let binary = '';
   109→            for (let i = 0; i < bytes.length; i++) {
   110→                binary += String.fromCharCode(bytes[i]);
   111→            }
   112→            const pdfBase64 = btoa(binary);
   113→
   114→            await fetchApi(`/protocols/${uploadResp.protocol_id}/confirm-upload`, {
   115→                method: 'POST',
   116→                body: JSON.stringify({ pdf_bytes_base64: pdfBase64 }),
   117→            });
   118→
   119→            return uploadResp;
   120→        },
   121→        onSuccess: () => {
   122→            queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['protocols'] });
   123→        },
   124→    });
   125→}
   126→
   127→export function useConfirmUpload() {
   128→    const queryClient = useQueryClient();
   129→
   130→    return useMutation({
   131→        mutationFn: ({ id, pdfBase64 }: { id: string; pdfBase64?: string }) =>
   132→            fetchApi(`/protocols/${id}/confirm-upload`, {
   133→                method: 'POST',
   134→                body: JSON.stringify(pdfBase64 ? { pdf_bytes_base64: pdfBase64 } : {}),
   135→            }),
   136→        onSuccess: () => {
   137→            queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['protocols'] });
   138→        },
   139→    });
   140→}
   141→
   142→export function useRetryProtocol() {
   143→    const queryClient = useQueryClient();
   144→
   145→    return useMutation({
   146→        mutationFn: (protocolId: string) =>
   147→            fetchApi<{ status: string; protocol_id: string }>(`/protocols/${protocolId}/retry`, {
   148→                method: 'POST',
   149→            }),
   150→        onSuccess: () => {
   151→            queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['protocols'] });
   152→        },
   153→    });
   154→}
   155→
   156→export interface ReExtractResponse {
   157→    status: string;
   158→    protocol_id: string;
   159→    archived_batches: number;
   160→}
   161→
   162→export function useReExtractProtocol() {
   163→    const queryClient = useQueryClient();
   164→
   165→    return useMutation({
   166→        mutationFn: (protocolId: string) =>
   167→            fetchApi<ReExtractResponse>(`/protocols/${protocolId}/re-extract`, {
   168→                method: 'POST',
   169→            }),
   170→        onSuccess: () => {
   171→            queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['protocols'] });
   172→            queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['review-batches'] });
   173→        },
   174→    });
   175→}
   176→
   177→export function useArchiveProtocol() {
   178→    const queryClient = useQueryClient();
   179→    return useMutation({
   180→        mutationFn: (protocolId: string) =>
   181→            fetchApi<Protocol>(`/protocols/${protocolId}/archive`, {
   182→                method: 'POST',
   183→            }),
   184→        onSuccess: () => {
   185→            queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['protocols'] });
   186→        },
   187→    });
   188→}

     1→import { toast } from 'sonner';
     2→
     3→import { useAuthStore } from '../stores/authStore';
     4→
     5→export const API_BASE_URL = import.meta.env.VITE_API_URL || 'http://localhost:8000';
     6→
     7→export class SessionExpiredError extends Error {
     8→    constructor() {
     9→        super('Session expired');
    10→        this.name = 'SessionExpiredError';
    11→    }
    12→}
    13→
    14→let _sessionExpiredToastShown = false;
    15→
    16→function _handleSessionExpired(): void {
    17→    useAuthStore.getState().setSessionExpired();
    18→    if (!_sessionExpiredToastShown) {
    19→        _sessionExpiredToastShown = true;
    20→        toast.error('Session expired — please sign in again', {
    21→            duration: 6000,
    22→            onDismiss: () => {
    23→                _sessionExpiredToastShown = false;
    24→            },
    25→            onAutoClose: () => {
    26→                _sessionExpiredToastShown = false;
    27→            },
    28→        });
    29→    }
    30→}
    31→
    32→/**
    33→ * Authenticated fetch wrapper. Attaches JWT and handles 401 → session expired.
    34→ */
    35→export async function fetchApi<T>(endpoint: string, options?: RequestInit): Promise<T> {
    36→    const token = useAuthStore.getState().token;
    37→    const headers: HeadersInit = {
    38→        'Content-Type': 'application/json',
    39→        ...(token ? { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } : {}),
    40→        ...(options?.headers || {}),
    41→    };
    42→
    43→    const response = await fetch(`${API_BASE_URL}${endpoint}`, {
    44→        ...options,
    45→        headers,
    46→    });
    47→
    48→    if (response.status === 401) {
    49→        _handleSessionExpired();
    50→        throw new SessionExpiredError();
    51→    }
    52→
    53→    if (!response.ok) {
    54→        throw new Error(`API Error: ${response.status} ${response.statusText}`);
    55→    }
    56→
    57→    return response.json();
    58→}
    59→
    60→/**
    61→ * Public (unauthenticated) fetch wrapper for endpoints like /health and /ready.
    62→ */
    63→export async function fetchPublicApi<T>(endpoint: string, options?: RequestInit): Promise<T> {
    64→    const response = await fetch(`${API_BASE_URL}${endpoint}`, {
    65→        ...options,
    66→        headers: {
    67→            'Content-Type': 'application/json',
    68→            ...options?.headers,
    69→        },
    70→    });
    71→
    72→    if (!response.ok) {
    73→        throw new Error(`API Error: ${response.status} ${response.statusText}`);
    74→    }
    75→
    76→    return response.json();
    77→}

> AGENT

The protocol dropdown is now populated and working. The issue was requesting `page_size=200` when the API enforces a max of 100 — changed to 100 and both protocols now appear in the selector.

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/apps/hitl-ui/src/components/criteria-table/FieldMappingCell.tsx",
  "old_string": "function formatValue(value: unknown, unit?: string): string {\n    if (value === null || value === undefined) return '';\n    if (typeof value === 'string' || typeof value === 'number') {\n        return unit ? `${value} ${unit}` : String(value);\n    }\n    if (typeof value === 'object') {\n        const v = value as Record<string, unknown>;\n        if (v.type === 'range') return `${v.min}–${v.max}${v.unit ? ` ${v.unit}` : ''}`;\n        if (v.type === 'temporal') return `${v.duration} ${v.unit}`;\n        if (v.type === 'standard') return `${v.value}${v.unit ? ` ${v.unit}` : ''}`;\n    }\n    return '';\n}",
  "new_string": "const MAX_VALUE_LEN = 80;\n\nfunction truncate(s: string): string {\n    return s.length > MAX_VALUE_LEN ? `${s.slice(0, MAX_VALUE_LEN)}…` : s;\n}\n\nfunction formatValue(value: unknown, unit?: string): string {\n    if (value === null || value === undefined) return '';\n    if (typeof value === 'string' || typeof value === 'number') {\n        return truncate(unit ? `${value} ${unit}` : String(value));\n    }\n    if (typeof value === 'object') {\n        const v = value as Record<string, unknown>;\n        if (v.type === 'range') return truncate(`${v.min}–${v.max}${v.unit ? ` ${v.unit}` : ''}`);\n        if (v.type === 'temporal') return truncate(`${v.duration} ${v.unit}`);\n        if (v.type === 'standard') return truncate(`${v.value}${v.unit ? ` ${v.unit}` : ''}`);\n    }\n    return '';\n}"
}

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/tools/field_mapper.py",
  "old_string": "    duration: str | None = Field(\n        default=None, description=\"Duration value for temporal type\"\n    )\n\n\n# The set of valid relation operators accepted by the frontend",
  "new_string": "    duration: str | None = Field(\n        default=None, description=\"Duration value for temporal type\"\n    )\n\n    @model_validator(mode=\"after\")\n    def truncate_long_strings(self) -> \"FieldMappingValue\":\n        \"\"\"Guard against LLM repetition loops producing absurdly long values.\"\"\"\n        _max = 200\n        for attr in (\"value\", \"unit\", \"min\", \"max\", \"duration\"):\n            val = getattr(self, attr)\n            if isinstance(val, str) and len(val) > _max:\n                setattr(self, attr, val[:_max])\n        return self\n\n\n# The set of valid relation operators accepted by the frontend"
}

```
     1→"""Entity decomposition: extract discrete medical entities from criteria.
     2→
     3→Criterion sentences like "eGFR >= 30 mL/min/1.73m2 or sCr <= 2.0 mg/dL" contain
     4→multiple groundable medical concepts. This tool decomposes them into discrete
     5→entities with correct types for TerminologyRouter dispatch.
     6→
     7→Uses the two-model architecture pattern: Gemini for structured output via
     8→LangChain's with_structured_output, matching the medgemma_decider.py pattern.
     9→"""
    10→
    11→from __future__ import annotations
    12→
    13→import logging
    14→import os
    15→from pathlib import Path
    16→from typing import Literal, cast
    17→
    18→from jinja2 import Environment, FileSystemLoader
    19→from langchain_google_genai import ChatGoogleGenerativeAI
    20→from pydantic import BaseModel, Field
    21→
    22→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    23→
    24→PROMPTS_DIR = Path(__file__).parent.parent / "prompts"
    25→
    26→
    27→class DecomposedEntity(BaseModel):
    28→    """A single medical entity extracted from a criterion sentence.
    29→
    30→    Attributes:
    31→        text: The specific medical term to ground (e.g. "eGFR", not the full sentence).
    32→        entity_type: Entity type matching routing.yaml keys exactly.
    33→    """
    34→
    35→    text: str = Field(description="The specific medical term to ground")
    36→    entity_type: Literal[
    37→        "Condition", "Medication", "Lab_Value", "Procedure", "Demographic", "Other"
    38→    ] = Field(description="Entity type for terminology routing")
    39→
    40→
    41→class DecomposedEntityList(BaseModel):
    42→    """List of decomposed entities from a single criterion sentence."""
    43→
    44→    entities: list[DecomposedEntity] = Field(default_factory=list)
    45→
    46→
    47→def _render_decompose_prompt(criterion_text: str, category: str | None) -> str:
    48→    """Render the entity decomposition Jinja2 prompt template.
    49→
    50→    Args:
    51→        criterion_text: Full criterion sentence to decompose.
    52→        category: Optional category hint from extraction (e.g. "lab_values").
    53→
    54→    Returns:
    55→        Rendered prompt string for Gemini.
    56→    """
    57→    env = Environment(loader=FileSystemLoader(str(PROMPTS_DIR)), autoescape=False)
    58→    template = env.get_template("entity_decompose.jinja2")
    59→    return template.render(criterion_text=criterion_text, category=category or "")
    60→
    61→
    62→async def decompose_entities_from_criterion(
    63→    criterion_text: str,
    64→    category: str | None,
    65→) -> list[dict]:
    66→    """Extract discrete medical entities from a criterion sentence.
    67→
    68→    Uses Gemini with structured output to decompose a criterion sentence
    69→    into individual medical terms with correct entity types. Falls back
    70→    to empty list on failure -- caller handles fallback to full-text entity.
    71→
    72→    Args:
    73→        criterion_text: The full criterion sentence to decompose.
    74→        category: Optional category hint from extraction.
    75→
    76→    Returns:
    77→        List of dicts with "text" and "entity_type" keys, or empty list on failure.
    78→    """
    79→    try:
    80→        gemini = ChatGoogleGenerativeAI(
    81→            model=os.getenv("GEMINI_MODEL_NAME", "gemini-2.5-flash"),
    82→            google_api_key=os.getenv("GOOGLE_API_KEY"),
    83→        )
    84→        from protocol_processor.tracing import llm_span
    85→
    86→        model_name = os.getenv("GEMINI_MODEL_NAME", "gemini-2.5-flash")
    87→        structured = gemini.with_structured_output(DecomposedEntityList)
    88→        prompt = _render_decompose_prompt(criterion_text, category)
    89→
    90→        with llm_span("gemini_entity_decompose", model_name) as llm:
    91→            llm.set_request(prompt)
    92→            result = await structured.ainvoke(prompt)
    93→            llm.set_response(str(result))
    94→
    95→        if isinstance(result, dict):
    96→            result = DecomposedEntityList.model_validate(result)
    97→        decomposed = cast(DecomposedEntityList, result)
    98→        return [e.model_dump() for e in decomposed.entities]
    99→    except Exception as e:
   100→        logger.error(
   101→            "Entity decomposition failed for criterion '%s': %s",
   102→            criterion_text[:80],
   103→            e,
   104→        )
   105→        return []  # Caller falls back to full-text entity
   106→
```

Now I see both root causes:

1. **Temporal hallucination**: The temporal example just says `"duration": "6", "unit": "months"` — there's no instruction saying "duration must be a plain number, NOT a prose description". The LLM interpreted "within the past 5 days and ongoing" and tried to describe the entire temporal context as the duration string.

2. **Age grounding failure**: Entity decomposer returns `[]` on any failure and swallows the exception. This is already-persisted data — the entities are empty in the DB.

Both are pipeline-side issues. Let me fix them properly:

```
     1→You are a clinical NLP entity extractor. Given a clinical trial eligibility criterion, extract ONLY the discrete medical concepts that should be grounded to terminology codes.
     2→
     3→## Rules
     4→1. Extract the medical ENTITY (not the full sentence). Examples:
     5→   - "eGFR >= 30 mL/min/1.73m2" -> text: "eGFR", entity_type: "Lab_Value"
     6→   - "No history of myocardial infarction" -> text: "myocardial infarction", entity_type: "Condition"
     7→   - "Currently taking calcium channel blockers" -> text: "calcium channel blockers", entity_type: "Medication"
     8→   - "Age 20 to 60 years" -> text: "age", entity_type: "Demographic"
     9→   - "BMI > 30 kg/m2" -> text: "body mass index", entity_type: "Lab_Value"
    10→   - "Coronary artery bypass graft within 3 months" -> text: "coronary artery bypass graft", entity_type: "Procedure"
    11→   - "Potassium >= 5.0 mmol/L" -> text: "potassium", entity_type: "Lab_Value"
    12→   - "LVEF <= 40%" -> text: "left ventricular ejection fraction", entity_type: "Lab_Value"
    13→
    14→2. One criterion may yield 0, 1, or multiple entities.
    15→3. Skip non-groundable concepts: consent, willingness, investigator discretion, geographic location, legal capacity.
    16→4. Entity types: Condition | Medication | Lab_Value | Procedure | Demographic | Other
    17→5. The `value` field is NOT used by this tool. Only extract `text` (the medical concept) and `entity_type`.
    18→6. Extract entities even from indication or context-framing criteria. The sentence structure does not matter — extract every medical concept regardless of how it is phrased.
    19→7. Any clinical scoring system, classification scale, physical status grade, or ordinal assessment is Lab_Value — never "Other". This includes ALL scales you know of, whether common or rare.
    20→8. Use "Other" ONLY as a last resort for truly unclassifiable entities. Always prefer a specific type. If in doubt between "Other" and a specific type, choose the specific type.
    21→
    22→## Medical Normalization Rules
    23→- Always normalize entity text to the standard medical NOUN form used in terminology systems (UMLS, SNOMED, ICD-10), not adjective/adverb forms from the criterion text. Adjective forms produce poor terminology search results:
    24→  - "immunocompromised" → "immunodeficiency" (Condition)
    25→  - "hypertensive" → "hypertension" (Condition)
    26→  - "febrile" → "fever" (Condition)
    27→- When a criterion describes a state resulting from a medical intervention, extract the INTERVENTION as a Procedure — not the resulting state as a Condition. In clinical trial eligibility language, these criteria require proof of a past procedure:
    28→  - "immunized against hepatitis B" → "hepatitis B vaccination" (Procedure)
    29→  - "must have undergone appendectomy" → "appendectomy" (Procedure)
    30→  - Prefer "-tion"/"-ectomy"/"-plasty" procedure nouns over "-ity"/"-ness" state nouns, as procedure nouns match terminology systems better and avoid ambiguity.
    31→- Do NOT embed demographic qualifiers (male/female/age) into clinical entity text — those belong as separate Demographic entities. Mixing demographics into clinical terms confuses terminology search:
    32→  - "Male patients with renal failure" → "renal failure" (Condition) AND "male" (Demographic)
    33→  - "Elderly subjects with osteoporosis" → "osteoporosis" (Condition) AND "age" (Demographic)
    34→
    35→## Compound Concept Rules — DO NOT Over-Decompose
    36→- Do NOT separate anatomical modifiers, severities, laterality, or procedure types from their parent concept. These are a single medical entity:
    37→  - "tricompartmental knee replacement" → ONE entity (not "knee replacement" + "tricompartmental")
    38→  - "spinal muscular atrophy" → ONE entity
    39→  - "non-small cell lung cancer" → ONE entity
    40→  - "left ventricular hypertrophy" → ONE entity
    41→  - "chronic obstructive pulmonary disease" → ONE entity
    42→  - "bilateral total hip arthroplasty" → ONE entity
    43→- DO separate distinct comorbidities or co-occurring conditions into individual entities:
    44→  - "Type 2 diabetes with nephropathy" → TWO entities: "type 2 diabetes" AND "nephropathy"
    45→  - "Hypertension and heart failure" → TWO entities: "hypertension" AND "heart failure"
    46→  - "Asthma or COPD" → TWO entities: "asthma" AND "chronic obstructive pulmonary disease"
    47→
    48→## Criterion
    49→{% if category %}Category hint: {{ category }}{% endif %}
    50→Text: "{{ criterion_text }}"
    51→
    52→Return a JSON object with key "entities" containing an array of {"text": string, "entity_type": string} objects.
    53→If no groundable entities exist, return {"entities": []}.
    54→
```

> AGENT

The template has `"Age 20 to 60 years" -> text: "age", entity_type: "Demographic"` as an example, so it should handle age. The issue is the `≥` unicode character. The Gemini call likely failed silently (exception caught on line 99).

The real fix is twofold: (1) better error logging so we can see WHY, and (2) normalize unicode operators in the criterion text before sending to the LLM.

> TOOL

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/tools/entity_decomposer.py",
  "old_string": "logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n\nPROMPTS_DIR = Path(__file__).parent.parent / \"prompts\"",
  "new_string": "logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n\nPROMPTS_DIR = Path(__file__).parent.parent / \"prompts\"\n\n# Unicode math symbols that Gemini/PDF extraction may produce.\n# Normalize to ASCII equivalents so the LLM template handles them cleanly.\n_UNICODE_OPERATORS: dict[str, str] = {\n    \"\\u2265\": \">=\",  # ≥\n    \"\\u2264\": \"<=\",  # ≤\n    \"\\u2260\": \"!=\",  # ≠\n    \"\\u00b1\": \"+-\",  # ±\n    \"\\u2013\": \"-\",   # en-dash\n    \"\\u2014\": \"-\",   # em-dash\n}\n\n\ndef _normalize_criterion_text(text: str) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Replace unicode math/comparison symbols with ASCII equivalents.\"\"\"\n    for char, repl in _UNICODE_OPERATORS.items():\n        text = text.replace(char, repl)\n    return text"
}

> AGENT

All clean. Here's what I found and fixed:

## Root Cause Analysis

### 1. COVID-19 temporal hallucination
**Cause**: The field_mapper prompt's temporal example was `{"duration": "6", "unit": "months"}` with no explicit instruction that `duration` must be a plain number. For "within the past 5 days and ongoing", the LLM tried to encode the entire temporal description as a hyphenated prose string in the `duration` field, then got stuck in a repetition loop producing `"5-days-ago-to-present-time-point-inclusive-of-current-time-point-of-patient-enrollment-..."` infinitely.

**Fix**: Added explicit instructions: `"duration MUST be a plain number (e.g. '5', '6', '30'). NEVER put prose in duration."` with a worked example for "within the past 5 days" and a BAD anti-pattern showing the exact failure mode. The truncation validator is still there as a safety net.

### 2. "Age ≥ 18 years" — zero entities extracted
**Cause**: The criterion text contains the unicode character `≥` (U+2265). The entity decomposer passes this directly to Gemini, which likely failed or returned empty results. The exception was silently swallowed (logged at error level but returning `[]`).

**Fix**: Added `_normalize_criterion_text()` that converts unicode math symbols (`≥ → >=`, `≤ → <=`, `≠ → !=`, en-dash → `-`, etc.) to ASCII before the LLM call. Also added a warning log when 0 entities are returned (not just on exceptions) and `exc_info=True` on the error path for full stack traces.
