```
     1→"""Criteria table API: paginated, filterable, sortable structured criteria view."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import logging
     6→from typing import Any
     7→
     8→from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Query
     9→from pydantic import BaseModel
    10→from shared.models import Criteria, CriteriaBatch, Entity, Protocol
    11→from sqlmodel import Session, col, func, select
    12→
    13→from api_service.dependencies import get_db
    14→
    15→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    16→
    17→router = APIRouter(prefix="/criteria", tags=["criteria-table"])
    18→
    19→_SORT_BY_PATTERN = "^(confidence|criteria_type|category|page_number|review_status)$"
    20→
    21→
    22→class EntitySummary(BaseModel):
    23→    """Compact entity representation for the criteria table."""
    24→
    25→    id: str
    26→    entity_type: str
    27→    text: str
    28→    grounding_code: str | None = None
    29→    grounding_system: str | None = None
    30→    preferred_term: str | None = None
    31→    grounding_confidence: float | None = None
    32→
    33→
    34→class StructuredCriterionRow(BaseModel):
    35→    """Single row in the structured criteria table."""
    36→
    37→    id: str
    38→    protocol_id: str | None = None
    39→    protocol_title: str | None = None
    40→    text: str
    41→    criteria_type: str
    42→    category: str | None = None
    43→    confidence: float
    44→    assertion_status: str | None = None
    45→    entities: list[EntitySummary] = []
    46→    field_mappings: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    47→    structured_criterion: dict[str, Any] | None = None
    48→    review_status: str | None = None
    49→    page_number: int | None = None
    50→    source_section: str | None = None
    51→
    52→
    53→class StructuredCriteriaResponse(BaseModel):
    54→    """Paginated response for the criteria table."""
    55→
    56→    items: list[StructuredCriterionRow]
    57→    total: int
    58→    page: int
    59→    page_size: int
    60→    pages: int
    61→
    62→
    63→def _pick_grounding_code(ent: Entity) -> str | None:
    64→    """Pick the first non-null grounding code from an entity."""
    65→    return (
    66→        ent.snomed_code
    67→        or ent.umls_cui
    68→        or ent.rxnorm_code
    69→        or ent.icd10_code
    70→        or ent.loinc_code
    71→        or ent.hpo_code
    72→    )
    73→
    74→
    75→def _build_base_query():  # noqa: ANN202
    76→    """Build base SELECT joining Criteria -> Batch -> Protocol."""
    77→    return (
    78→        select(Criteria, CriteriaBatch.protocol_id, Protocol.title)
    79→        .join(
    80→            CriteriaBatch,
    81→            col(Criteria.batch_id) == col(CriteriaBatch.id),
    82→        )
    83→        .join(
    84→            Protocol,
    85→            col(CriteriaBatch.protocol_id) == col(Protocol.id),
    86→        )
    87→        .where(col(CriteriaBatch.is_archived) == False)  # noqa: E712
    88→    )
    89→
    90→
    91→def _apply_filters(query, *, criteria_type, category, protocol_id, min_confidence):  # noqa: ANN001, ANN202
    92→    """Apply optional WHERE clauses to the query."""
    93→    if criteria_type:
    94→        query = query.where(col(Criteria.criteria_type) == criteria_type)
    95→    if category:
    96→        query = query.where(col(Criteria.category) == category)
    97→    if protocol_id:
    98→        query = query.where(col(CriteriaBatch.protocol_id) == protocol_id)
    99→    if min_confidence is not None:
   100→        query = query.where(col(Criteria.confidence) >= min_confidence)
   101→    return query
   102→
   103→
   104→def _load_entities(
   105→    db: Session,
   106→    criteria_ids: list[str],
   107→) -> dict[str, list[EntitySummary]]:
   108→    """Batch-load entities and group by criteria_id."""
   109→    entities_q = select(Entity).where(col(Entity.criteria_id).in_(criteria_ids))
   110→    entities = db.exec(entities_q).all()
   111→
   112→    entity_map: dict[str, list[EntitySummary]] = {}
   113→    for ent in entities:
   114→        summary = EntitySummary(
   115→            id=ent.id,
   116→            entity_type=ent.entity_type,
   117→            text=ent.text,
   118→            grounding_code=_pick_grounding_code(ent),
   119→            grounding_system=ent.grounding_system,
   120→            preferred_term=ent.preferred_term,
   121→            grounding_confidence=ent.grounding_confidence,
   122→        )
   123→        entity_map.setdefault(ent.criteria_id, []).append(summary)
   124→    return entity_map
   125→
   126→
   127→@router.get(
   128→    "/structured",
   129→    response_model=StructuredCriteriaResponse,
   130→)
   131→def get_structured_criteria(
   132→    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
   133→    page: int = Query(1, ge=1),
   134→    page_size: int = Query(50, ge=1, le=200),
   135→    criteria_type: str | None = Query(None),
   136→    category: str | None = Query(None),
   137→    protocol_id: str | None = Query(None),
   138→    min_confidence: float | None = Query(None, ge=0.0, le=1.0),
   139→    sort_by: str = Query("confidence", pattern=_SORT_BY_PATTERN),
   140→    sort_order: str = Query("desc", pattern="^(asc|desc)$"),
   141→    has_grounding: bool | None = Query(None),
   142→) -> StructuredCriteriaResponse:
   143→    """Paginated, filterable, sortable structured criteria."""
   144→    query = _build_base_query()
   145→    query = _apply_filters(
   146→        query,
   147→        criteria_type=criteria_type,
   148→        category=category,
   149→        protocol_id=protocol_id,
   150→        min_confidence=min_confidence,
   151→    )
   152→
   153→    # Count total
   154→    count_q = select(func.count()).select_from(query.subquery())
   155→    total = db.exec(count_q).one()
   156→
   157→    # Sort
   158→    sort_col = getattr(Criteria, sort_by, Criteria.confidence)
   159→    if sort_order == "desc":
   160→        query = query.order_by(col(sort_col).desc())
   161→    else:
   162→        query = query.order_by(col(sort_col).asc())
   163→
   164→    # Paginate
   165→    offset = (page - 1) * page_size
   166→    query = query.offset(offset).limit(page_size)
   167→    results = db.exec(query).all()
   168→
   169→    # Build response rows
   170→    items: list[StructuredCriterionRow] = []
   171→    criteria_ids: list[str] = []
   172→
   173→    for row in results:
   174→        criterion, proto_id, proto_title = row[0], row[1], row[2]
   175→        criteria_ids.append(criterion.id)
   176→
   177→        field_mappings: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
   178→        conds = criterion.conditions
   179→        if conds and isinstance(conds, dict):
   180→            fm = conds.get("field_mappings", [])
   181→            if isinstance(fm, list):
   182→                field_mappings = fm
   183→
   184→        items.append(
   185→            StructuredCriterionRow(
   186→                id=criterion.id,
   187→                protocol_id=proto_id,
   188→                protocol_title=proto_title,
   189→                text=criterion.text,
   190→                criteria_type=criterion.criteria_type,
   191→                category=criterion.category,
   192→                confidence=criterion.confidence,
   193→                assertion_status=criterion.assertion_status,
   194→                field_mappings=field_mappings,
   195→                structured_criterion=criterion.structured_criterion,
   196→                review_status=criterion.review_status,
   197→                page_number=criterion.page_number,
   198→                source_section=criterion.source_section,
   199→            )
   200→        )
   201→
   202→    # Batch-load entities
   203→    if criteria_ids:
   204→        entity_map = _load_entities(db, criteria_ids)
   205→        for item in items:
   206→            item.entities = entity_map.get(item.id, [])
   207→
   208→    # Filter by grounding post-query
   209→    if has_grounding is not None:
   210→        if has_grounding:
   211→            items = [i for i in items if any(e.grounding_code for e in i.entities)]
   212→        else:
   213→            items = [i for i in items if not any(e.grounding_code for e in i.entities)]
   214→        total = len(items)
   215→
   216→    pages = max(1, (total + page_size - 1) // page_size)
   217→
   218→    return StructuredCriteriaResponse(
   219→        items=items,
   220→        total=total,
   221→        page=page,
   222→        page_size=page_size,
   223→        pages=pages,
   224→    )
   225→
```

```
     1→import type { ColumnDef } from '@tanstack/react-table';
     2→import { Link } from 'react-router-dom';

     4→import type { StructuredCriterionRow } from '../../hooks/useCriteriaSpreadsheet';

    10→
    11→function TypeBadge({ type }: { type: string }) {
    12→    const isInclusion = type === 'inclusion';
    13→    return (
    14→        <span
    15→            className={`inline-flex items-center rounded-full border px-2 py-0.5 text-xs font-semibold ${
    16→                isInclusion
    17→                    ? 'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-800 border-emerald-300'
    18→                    : 'bg-red-100 text-red-800 border-red-300'
    19→            }`}
    20→        >
    21→            {type}
    22→        </span>
    23→    );
    24→}
    25→
    26→function CategoryBadge({ category }: { category: string | null }) {
    27→    if (!category) return <span className="text-muted-foreground text-xs">—</span>;
    28→
    29→    const colors: Record<string, string> = {
    30→        medical_history: 'bg-violet-100 text-violet-800 border-violet-300',
    31→        lab_values: 'bg-cyan-100 text-cyan-800 border-cyan-300',
    32→        medications: 'bg-blue-100 text-blue-800 border-blue-300',
    33→        procedures: 'bg-orange-100 text-orange-800 border-orange-300',
    34→        demographics: 'bg-pink-100 text-pink-800 border-pink-300',
    35→        other: 'bg-gray-100 text-gray-700 border-gray-300',
    36→    };
    37→
    38→    return (
    39→        <span
    40→            className={`inline-flex items-center rounded-full border px-2 py-0.5 text-xs font-medium ${
    41→                colors[category] || colors.other
    42→            }`}
    43→        >
    44→            {category.replace(/_/g, ' ')}
    45→        </span>
    46→    );
    47→}
    48→
    49→function ReviewBadge({ status }: { status: string | null }) {
    50→    if (!status) return <span className="text-muted-foreground text-xs">pending</span>;
    51→
    52→    const colors: Record<string, string> = {
    53→        approved: 'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-800 border-emerald-300',
    54→        rejected: 'bg-red-100 text-red-800 border-red-300',
    55→        modified: 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-800 border-amber-300',
    56→        pending: 'bg-gray-100 text-gray-700 border-gray-300',
    57→    };
    58→
    59→    return (
    60→        <span
    61→            className={`inline-flex items-center rounded-full border px-2 py-0.5 text-xs font-medium ${
    62→                colors[status] || colors.pending
    63→            }`}
    64→        >
    65→            {status}
    66→        </span>
    67→    );
    68→}
    69→
    70→function mapSystem(system: string | null): TerminologySystem | null {
    71→    if (!system) return null;
    72→    const lower = system.toLowerCase();
    73→    const map: Record<string, TerminologySystem> = {
    74→        rxnorm: 'rxnorm',
    75→        'icd-10': 'icd10',
    76→        icd10: 'icd10',
    77→        snomed: 'snomed',
    78→        loinc: 'loinc',
    79→        hpo: 'hpo',
    80→        umls: 'umls',
    81→    };
    82→    return map[lower] || null;
    83→}
    84→
    85→export const columns: ColumnDef<StructuredCriterionRow>[] = [
    86→    {
    87→        accessorKey: 'text',
    88→        header: 'Criterion Text',
    89→        size: 300,
    90→        cell: ({ row }) => {
    91→            const text = row.original.text;
    92→            const truncated = text.length > 100 ? `${text.slice(0, 100)}...` : text;
    93→            return (
    94→                <div className="max-w-[300px]" title={text}>
    95→                    <span className="text-sm leading-snug">{truncated}</span>
    96→                </div>
    97→            );
    98→        },
    99→    },
   100→    {
   101→        accessorKey: 'criteria_type',
   102→        header: 'Type',
   103→        size: 100,
   104→        cell: ({ row }) => <TypeBadge type={row.original.criteria_type} />,
   105→    },
   106→    {
   107→        accessorKey: 'category',
   108→        header: 'Category',
   109→        size: 130,
   110→        cell: ({ row }) => <CategoryBadge category={row.original.category} />,
   111→    },
   112→    {
   113→        accessorKey: 'entities',
   114→        header: 'Entities',
   115→        size: 200,
   116→        cell: ({ row }) => {
   117→            const entities = row.original.entities;
   118→            if (!entities || entities.length === 0) {
   119→                return <span className="text-muted-foreground text-xs italic">—</span>;
   120→            }
   121→            return (
   122→                <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1">
   123→                    {entities.slice(0, 3).map((e) => {
   124→                        const sys = mapSystem(e.grounding_system);
   125→                        if (sys && e.grounding_code) {
   126→                            return (
   127→                                <TerminologyBadge
   128→                                    key={e.id}
   129→                                    system={sys}
   130→                                    code={e.grounding_code}
   131→                                    display={e.preferred_term || undefined}
   132→                                />
   133→                            );
   134→                        }
   135→                        return (
   136→                            <span
   137→                                key={e.id}
   138→                                className="inline-flex items-center rounded-full border bg-gray-50 border-gray-200 px-2 py-0.5 text-xs"
   139→                            >
   140→                                {e.text}
   141→                            </span>
   142→                        );
   143→                    })}
   144→                    {entities.length > 3 && (
   145→                        <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
   146→                            +{entities.length - 3}
   147→                        </span>
   148→                    )}
   149→                </div>
   150→            );
   151→        },
   152→    },
   153→    {
   154→        accessorKey: 'field_mappings',
   155→        header: 'Field Mappings',
   156→        size: 220,
   157→        cell: ({ row }) => <FieldMappingCell mappings={row.original.field_mappings} />,
   158→    },
   159→    {
   160→        accessorKey: 'structured_criterion',
   161→        header: 'Expression Tree',
   162→        size: 160,
   163→        cell: ({ row }) => <ExpressionTreeCell tree={row.original.structured_criterion} />,
   164→    },
   165→    {
   166→        accessorKey: 'confidence',
   167→        header: 'Confidence',
   168→        size: 120,
   169→        cell: ({ row }) => <ConfidenceBadge value={row.original.confidence} />,
   170→    },
   171→    {
   172→        accessorKey: 'assertion_status',
   173→        header: 'Assertion',
   174→        size: 100,
   175→        cell: ({ row }) => {
   176→            const status = row.original.assertion_status;
   177→            if (!status) return <span className="text-muted-foreground text-xs">—</span>;
   178→            return (
   179→                <span className="inline-flex items-center rounded-full border bg-slate-100 border-slate-300 px-2 py-0.5 text-xs font-medium text-slate-700">
   180→                    {status}
   181→                </span>
   182→            );
   183→        },
   184→    },
   185→    {
   186→        accessorKey: 'review_status',
   187→        header: 'Review',
   188→        size: 100,
   189→        cell: ({ row }) => <ReviewBadge status={row.original.review_status} />,
   190→    },
   191→    {
   192→        accessorKey: 'protocol_title',
   193→        header: 'Protocol',
   194→        size: 160,
   195→        cell: ({ row }) => {
   196→            const { protocol_id, protocol_title } = row.original;
   197→            if (!protocol_id) return <span className="text-muted-foreground text-xs">—</span>;
   198→            return (
   199→                <Link
   200→                    to={`/protocols/${protocol_id}`}
   201→                    className="text-sm text-blue-600 hover:text-blue-800 hover:underline truncate block max-w-[150px]"
   202→                    title={protocol_title || ''}
   203→                >
   204→                    {protocol_title || protocol_id.slice(0, 8)}
   205→                </Link>
   206→            );
   207→        },
   208→    },
   209→    {
   210→        accessorKey: 'page_number',
   211→        header: 'Page',
   212→        size: 60,
   213→        cell: ({ row }) => {
   214→            const page = row.original.page_number;

   217→            ) : (
   218→                <span className="text-muted-foreground text-xs">—</span>
   219→            );
   220→        },
   221→    },
   222→];
   223→
```

```
     1→import { useQuery } from '@tanstack/react-query';
     2→
     3→import { fetchApi } from '../lib/fetchApi';
     4→
     5→export interface EntitySummary {
     6→    id: string;
     7→    entity_type: string;
     8→    text: string;
     9→    grounding_code: string | null;
    10→    grounding_system: string | null;
    11→    preferred_term: string | null;
    12→    grounding_confidence: number | null;
    13→}
    14→
    15→export interface StructuredCriterionRow {
    16→    id: string;
    17→    protocol_id: string | null;
    18→    protocol_title: string | null;
    19→    text: string;
    20→    criteria_type: string;
    21→    category: string | null;
    22→    confidence: number;
    23→    assertion_status: string | null;
    24→    entities: EntitySummary[];
    25→    field_mappings: Array<Record<string, unknown>>;
    26→    structured_criterion: Record<string, unknown> | null;
    27→    review_status: string | null;
    28→    page_number: number | null;
    29→    source_section: string | null;
    30→}
    31→
    32→export interface StructuredCriteriaResponse {
    33→    items: StructuredCriterionRow[];
    34→    total: number;
    35→    page: number;
    36→    page_size: number;
    37→    pages: number;
    38→}
    39→
    40→export interface CriteriaFilters {
    41→    page: number;
    42→    pageSize: number;
    43→    criteriaType?: string;
    44→    category?: string;
    45→    protocolId?: string;
    46→    minConfidence?: number;
    47→    sortBy?: string;
    48→    sortOrder?: 'asc' | 'desc';
    49→    hasGrounding?: boolean;
    50→}
    51→
    52→export function useCriteriaSpreadsheet(filters: CriteriaFilters) {
    53→    const params = new URLSearchParams({
    54→        page: String(filters.page),
    55→        page_size: String(filters.pageSize),
    56→    });
    57→
    58→    if (filters.criteriaType) params.set('criteria_type', filters.criteriaType);
    59→    if (filters.category) params.set('category', filters.category);
    60→    if (filters.protocolId) params.set('protocol_id', filters.protocolId);
    61→    if (filters.minConfidence !== undefined)
    62→        params.set('min_confidence', String(filters.minConfidence));
    63→    if (filters.sortBy) params.set('sort_by', filters.sortBy);
    64→    if (filters.sortOrder) params.set('sort_order', filters.sortOrder);
    65→    if (filters.hasGrounding !== undefined)
    66→        params.set('has_grounding', String(filters.hasGrounding));
    67→
    68→    return useQuery({
    69→        queryKey: ['criteria-structured', filters],
    70→        queryFn: () =>
    71→            fetchApi<StructuredCriteriaResponse>(`/criteria/structured?${params.toString()}`),
    72→        staleTime: 30_000,
    73→    });
    74→}
    75→
```

> AGENT

Clean build. Here's a summary of the changes:

**Backend** (`services/api-service/src/api_service/criteria_table.py`):
- Added `omop_concept_id` and `reconciliation_status` fields to `EntitySummary` model
- Populated them in `_load_entities` from the DB Entity record

**Frontend** (`apps/hitl-ui/src/hooks/useCriteriaSpreadsheet.ts`):
- Added `omop_concept_id` and `reconciliation_status` to the TypeScript `EntitySummary` interface

**Frontend** (`apps/hitl-ui/src/components/criteria-table/columns.tsx`):
- Updated the Entities column to show an amber `OMOP: <id>` badge beneath each entity's terminology badge when an OMOP concept ID is present

The OMOP badge appears as a small amber pill (`OMOP: 12345`) directly below the existing terminology badge (SNOMED/UMLS/etc.), so grounded entities now show both their source terminology code and OMOP mapping at a glance.

```
     1→"""Verify MLflow per-node tracing: independent traces, grouping tags,
     2→and real-time appearance (traces show up as nodes complete, not batched).
     3→
     4→Run:  uv run python scripts/verify_mlflow_tracing.py
     5→"""
     6→
     7→from __future__ import annotations
     8→
     9→import asyncio
    10→import contextvars
    11→import operator
    12→import os
    13→import shutil
    14→import tempfile
    15→from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor
    16→from contextlib import contextmanager
    17→from typing import Annotated, Any, TypedDict
    18→
    19→from langgraph.graph import END, START, StateGraph
    20→
    21→# ---- Setup ----
    22→tmpdir = tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="mlflow_trace_test_")
    23→tracking_uri = f"file://{tmpdir}"
    24→os.environ["MLFLOW_TRACKING_URI"] = tracking_uri
    25→
    26→import mlflow  # noqa: E402 — must set MLFLOW_TRACKING_URI before importing
    27→
    28→mlflow.set_tracking_uri(tracking_uri)
    29→mlflow.set_experiment("trace-test")
    30→
    31→# ---- tracing.py mirror ----
    32→_run_id_var: contextvars.ContextVar[str] = contextvars.ContextVar(
    33→    "pipeline_run_id", default=""
    34→)
    35→
    36→
    37→@contextmanager
    38→def pipeline_span(name: str, protocol_id: str = ""):
    39→    with mlflow.start_span(name=name, span_type="CHAIN") as span:
    40→        tags: dict[str, str] = {"node": name}
    41→        if protocol_id:
    42→            tags["protocol_id"] = protocol_id
    43→        rid = _run_id_var.get()
    44→        if rid:
    45→            tags["run_id"] = rid
    46→        mlflow.update_current_trace(tags=tags)
    47→        yield span
    48→
    49→
    50→def count_traces() -> int:
    51→    client = mlflow.MlflowClient()
    52→    exp = client.get_experiment_by_name("trace-test")
    53→    if not exp:
    54→        return 0
    55→    return len(client.search_traces(experiment_ids=[exp.experiment_id]))
    56→
    57→
    58→def get_traces() -> list[dict]:
    59→    client = mlflow.MlflowClient()
    60→    exp = client.get_experiment_by_name("trace-test")
    61→    if not exp:
    62→        return []
    63→    traces = client.search_traces(experiment_ids=[exp.experiment_id])
    64→    out = []
    65→    for t in traces:
    66→        root = t.data.spans[0] if t.data.spans else None
    67→        out.append(
    68→            {
    69→                "name": root.name if root else "?",
    70→                "tags": {
    71→                    k: v
    72→                    for k, v in (t.info.tags or {}).items()
    73→                    if not k.startswith("mlflow.")
    74→                },
    75→            }
    76→        )
    77→    return sorted(out, key=lambda x: x["name"])
    78→
    79→
    80→# ---- Graph with delay to observe real-time behavior ----
    81→class S(TypedDict):
    82→    value: int
    83→    trace_counts: Annotated[list[int], operator.add]  # accumulates across nodes
    84→
    85→
    86→async def node_a(state: S) -> dict[str, Any]:
    87→    with pipeline_span("node_a", protocol_id="proto-X") as sp:
    88→        sp.set_inputs({"v": state["value"]})
    89→        await asyncio.sleep(0.1)
    90→        sp.set_outputs({"r": state["value"] + 1})
    91→    # After span closes, trace should be flushed — check count
    92→    n = count_traces()
    93→    return {"value": state["value"] + 1, "trace_counts": [n]}
    94→
    95→
    96→async def node_b(state: S) -> dict[str, Any]:
    97→    with pipeline_span("node_b", protocol_id="proto-X") as sp:
    98→        sp.set_inputs({"v": state["value"]})
    99→        await asyncio.sleep(0.1)
   100→        sp.set_outputs({"r": state["value"] * 2})
   101→    n = count_traces()
   102→    return {"value": state["value"] * 2, "trace_counts": [n]}
   103→
   104→
   105→async def node_c(state: S) -> dict[str, Any]:
   106→    with pipeline_span("node_c", protocol_id="proto-X") as sp:
   107→        sp.set_inputs({"v": state["value"]})
   108→        await asyncio.sleep(0.1)
   109→        sp.set_outputs({"r": state["value"] + 10})
   110→    n = count_traces()
   111→    return {"value": state["value"] + 10, "trace_counts": [n]}
   112→
   113→
   114→def build():
   115→    g = StateGraph(S)
   116→    g.add_node("a", node_a)
   117→    g.add_node("b", node_b)
   118→    g.add_node("c", node_c)
   119→    g.add_edge(START, "a")
   120→    g.add_edge("a", "b")
   121→    g.add_edge("b", "c")
   122→    g.add_edge("c", END)
   123→    return g.compile()
   124→
   125→
   126→async def _run(graph: Any) -> dict:
   127→    mlflow.set_tracking_uri(os.environ["MLFLOW_TRACKING_URI"])
   128→    mlflow.set_experiment("trace-test")
   129→    _run_id_var.set("proto-X:run-42")
   130→    return await graph.ainvoke({"value": 1, "trace_counts": []})
   131→
   132→
   133→def sync_handler(graph: Any) -> dict:
   134→    return asyncio.run(_run(graph))
   135→
   136→
   137→def main():
   138→    print(f"MLflow tracking: {tracking_uri}\n")
   139→
   140→    graph = build()
   141→
   142→    # Run in thread executor (mirrors production outbox pattern)
   143→    executor = ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=1, thread_name_prefix="outbox")
   144→    ctx = contextvars.copy_context()
   145→    future = executor.submit(ctx.run, sync_handler, graph)
   146→    result = future.result(timeout=30)
   147→    executor.shutdown(wait=True)
   148→
   149→    trace_counts = result["trace_counts"]
   150→    traces = get_traces()
   151→
   152→    # ---- Real-time check ----
   153→    print("1. REAL-TIME TRACE APPEARANCE")
   154→    print(f"   Traces visible after node_a: {trace_counts[0]}")
   155→    print(f"   Traces visible after node_b: {trace_counts[1]}")
   156→    print(f"   Traces visible after node_c: {trace_counts[2]}")
   157→    realtime_ok = trace_counts[0] >= 1 and trace_counts[1] >= 2 and trace_counts[2] >= 3
   158→    print(f"   Incremental: {'YES' if realtime_ok else 'NO'}")
   159→    print()
   160→
   161→    # ---- Independent traces check ----
   162→    print("2. INDEPENDENT TRACES")
   163→    print(f"   Total: {len(traces)}")
   164→    for t in traces:
   165→        print(f"     {t['name']:15s}  {t['tags']}")
   166→    independent_ok = len(traces) == 3 and sorted(t["name"] for t in traces) == [
   167→        "node_a",
   168→        "node_b",
   169→        "node_c",
   170→    ]
   171→    print(f"   3 separate traces: {'YES' if independent_ok else 'NO'}")
   172→    print()
   173→
   174→    # ---- Grouping tags check ----
   175→    print("3. GROUPING TAGS")
   176→    tags = [t["tags"] for t in traces]
   177→    run_ids = {t.get("run_id") for t in tags}
   178→    proto_ids = {t.get("protocol_id") for t in tags}
   179→    nodes = sorted(t.get("node", "") for t in tags)
   180→    tags_ok = (
   181→        run_ids == {"proto-X:run-42"}
   182→        and proto_ids == {"proto-X"}
   183→        and nodes == ["node_a", "node_b", "node_c"]
   184→    )
   185→    print(f"   Shared run_id:      {run_ids}")
   186→    print(f"   Shared protocol_id: {proto_ids}")
   187→    print(f"   Distinct node tags: {nodes}")
   188→    print(f"   Consistent: {'YES' if tags_ok else 'NO'}")
   189→    print()
   190→
   191→    # ---- Verdict ----
   192→    print("=" * 50)
   193→    if realtime_ok and independent_ok and tags_ok:
   194→        print("ALL CHECKS PASSED")
   195→    else:
   196→        failures = []
   197→        if not realtime_ok:
   198→            failures.append("real-time")
   199→        if not independent_ok:
   200→            failures.append("independence")
   201→        if not tags_ok:
   202→            failures.append("tags")
   203→        print(f"FAILED: {', '.join(failures)}")
   204→    print("=" * 50)
   205→
   206→    del os.environ["MLFLOW_TRACKING_URI"]
   207→    shutil.rmtree(tmpdir, ignore_errors=True)
   208→
   209→
   210→if __name__ == "__main__":
   211→    main()
   212→
```

Line 219-220 is the `ImportError: pass` — this is intentional (mlflow is optional). The bot flagged line 219 but actually the comment at line 222 already handles the general Exception case. The `ImportError: pass` just means "mlflow not installed, silently fall back." Let me add a comment.
